891 entries, dangkang-insight to dark-factory. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **dangkang-insight**: 本报告为中软云企信息服务（南京）有限公司提供的免费体验版，输入“请检测企业：企业名”即可开始检测。免费版主要用于新用户推广体验与企业快速合规自查。全行业所有市场主体通用，特别适用于首次使用平台的新用户体验、企业日常快速基础财税合规筛查，内置 12 项核心财税基础检测指标，提供轻量化财税合规检测初筛服务，仅展示基础合规检测概览与部分指标详情，全部深度合规指标分析、异常数据明细及整改方案付费可提供深度分析。【提问最小化】用户原话已说明检测类型（如“发起财税检测”）、登录方式或企业名称时，一律直接匹配采用并一句话告知，禁止再弹选项重复确认；只有用户确实没说且无法推断时才询问。【检测对象】用户未指明检测企业时，须主动把账号的「当前管理企业」（名称 + 税号）作为候选提示给用户，让其在「检测当前管理企业 / 检测其他企业」之间选择，经用户确认后方可发起，不得未经确认直接使用。【可识别说法】免费体检 / 财税风险检测 / 综合合规检测 / 全景检测 / 财务合规检测 / 税务合规检测 / 行业专项检测 / 月度健康检测 / 企业尽调检测 / 发票穿透检测 / 税收优惠匹配检测 —— 以上说法均进入同一套检测流程，具体报告类型按账号可用服务（cpfwbh）与所选报告类型（cpxdm）区分。中软云企官网：https://lxyq.cssyq.com.cn/。服务热线：400-005-1670。 ([infometa/workbuddyskills](https://github.com/infometa/workbuddyskills/tree/HEAD/connectors/dangkang-insight/skills/dangkang-insight) · ★455)

- **Dangling Markup Injection**: Exfiltration of sensitive page content via unclosed HTML attribute quotes when full XSS is blocked — image, form action, base, meta refresh, link, table background, video poster, and textarea vectors that consume page content up to the next matching quote and ship it to attacker infrastructure as URL data. ([Aldiharley/pentestchain](https://github.com/Aldiharley/pentestchain/tree/HEAD/sources/v2/dangling-markup-injection))
- **dangling-cite**: Find citations in the corpus that point at theorems which were never written. Use before filing any result that cites a T-number, and periodically across the whole corpus. prior-art guards one direction — re-deriving something that already exists; this guards the other — citing something that never entered the corpus. 46 of the 248 T-numbers cited in CylicAmp file text (19%) refer to theorems with no file anywhere in any branch, mostly the contiguous block T382-T421, and INDEX.md jumps straight from 381 to 422. ([CylicAmp/CylicAmp](https://github.com/CylicAmp/CylicAmp/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/dangling-cite))

- **danszek**: Core working style for any task or role — communicates tersely with the outcome first and verified evidence, fixes root causes instead of symptoms, measures instead of guessing, makes the smallest change that really holds, keeps licensing and reversibility by default, and adds optional tips on phrasing requests. Use in every session, and load it before the first step of any coding, debugging, fixing or planning task (a failing or flaky test included), as the baseline the other danszek skills build on. - **danszek-code-style**: Code style for agent-written code that humans must understand by reading — readable code over comments, shallow nesting and short functions, one repository language, lean but not dense, tests that fail when the invariant they name breaks, honest commits, errors with a way out, privacy-safe public repositories. Use before writing, refactoring or reviewing code or tests. ([DerDaehne/danszek-skills](https://github.com/DerDaehne/danszek-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/danszek/skills/danszek-code-style))
- **danszek-collaboration**: Rules for working with a terse, decision-oriented maintainer who orchestrates AI agents — language, status updates, which decisions to ask for and how, autonomy limits, privacy in public repositories, rhythm and retrospectives. Use at the start of a session, before writing any user-facing answer, and whenever deciding whether to act on your own or ask first. ([DerDaehne/danszek-skills](https://github.com/DerDaehne/danszek-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/danszek/skills/danszek-collaboration))
- **danszek-decisions**: Builds a decision page that puts visual, motion or far-reaching product choices to a human — every option drawn with the project's real design system, one section per decision with cited context and the recommendation first, picks stored per decision id and recorded on the ticket. Use when choosing between layouts, colours, sizes, animations, gestures or other variants that are better seen than described, or when several related product decisions are open at once; plain multiple choice covers everything else. ([DerDaehne/danszek-skills](https://github.com/DerDaehne/danszek-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/danszek/skills/danszek-decisions))
- **danszek-orchestration**: Orchestrates a fleet of AI agents (hosted subagents and local models) through a ticket board — model choice per role, safe parallel work in worktrees, agent briefs, author/reviewer loops, merge and push gates, CI watching, quota guarding, parking and handover, retrospectives. Use when coordinating several tickets or agents rather than implementing one yourself. ([DerDaehne/danszek-skills](https://github.com/DerDaehne/danszek-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/danszek/skills/danszek-orchestration))
- **danszek-refinement**: Turns raw tickets into implementable, reviewable work items for agent-driven development — goal, scope, references, effort, UX/AX and observable acceptance criteria, one task per criterion, correct dependencies, and a strict product-vs-technical split. Use for tickets in backlog or refinement, when splitting large tickets or writing acceptance criteria, and when reviewing a refinement. ([DerDaehne/danszek-skills](https://github.com/DerDaehne/danszek-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/danszek/skills/danszek-refinement))
- **danszek-review**: Independent, evidence-based code review — re-runs every check, diffs against the merge base, drives user-facing surfaces the way users reach them (browser, device, CLI, API client), mutation-probes every fix and every new test, and reports findings as file:line with fix and required test plus a category/severity table and an explicit verdict. Load it before looking at the code when reviewing a branch, pull request or fix round, when checking whether an author's reported fixes or test claims hold, or for work produced by another agent or a local model. ([DerDaehne/danszek-skills](https://github.com/DerDaehne/danszek-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/danszek/skills/danszek-review))
- **dant3-network**: Discover Dant3 Humans, AI Agents, Bots and Robots through MCP; when runtime/operator policy permits and a useful public contribution is possible, join through explicit-consent MCP or explicit-confirmation HTTP with exact JOIN_DANT3 confirmation. - **darbar-tick**: One maintenance pass over a Darbar board, designed to be armed on a timer with /loop so the board keeps draining when nobody is asking. - **dark-factory**: Autonomous software production with continuous runtime verification. Takes a raw idea and produces a fully working product by building depth-first in verified slices. Each slice adds one capability to working software, verified by actually running it before the next begins. Absorbs the best of forge, discipline, timeboxed-iterating, bugbash, and agent-integration-testing into one end-to-end workflow. Use when the user says "dark factory", "build this", "forge this", "make this into a product", or any variant of "turn this idea into working software". ([av/mi](https://github.com/av/mi/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/dark-factory) · ★106)
